BLUE KHATA — AI_NOTES  (aakhri: v2.95.5, 2026-10-06 — neeche sab se aakhir mein)
Yeh file agli AI ke liye hai: pichhli chat mein kya kaam hua.

=== MASLA (malik ne bataya) ===
POS purchase bill (shop PC se sync) aur larke ki photo wali CASH purchase
aapas mein "khud milao" nahi ho rahe the. Purchase page par "1 POS bill abhi
milane hain" banner to aata tha, magar kai bill par "Milao" button hi nazar
nahi aata tha. Natija: ek hi kharid do dafa dikhti thi (11,520 / 34,920 /
10,800 · 2026-09-20).

=== TASHKHEES (app.js ke purane code se) ===
1. cashCandidates() sirf WOHI cash purchase uthata tha jiska partyId POS bill
   ke partyId ke bilkul barabar ho, aur 3 din ke andar ho.
   Screenshots mein "Saim purchaser", "SAIM purchaser" aur "Saim default
   purchase akhrajat and sale" ALAG-ALAG party records hain.
   >>> MALIK NE CONFIRM KIYA: yeh sab ALAG suppliers hain. Is liye party
   >>> MERGE / naam se auto-matching JAAN BUJH KAR NAHI ki gayi. Agli AI bhi
   >>> party merge na kare.
2. autoLinkPurchases() sirf renderPurchases() se chalta tha (Purchase page).
   Party ke khate (ledger) par kuch nahi hota tha.
3. autoLink ka catch{} khali tha: nakami (permission-denied waghera) khamoshi
   se gum ho jati thi, aur autoLinkTried mein id pehle hi dal di jati thi, to
   page refresh tak dobara koshish bhi nahi hoti thi.
4. "Milao" button sirf tab render hota tha jab candidate mil jaye — candidate
   na mile to malik ke paas haath se jorne ka koi rasta hi nahi bachta tha.

=== v1.79.2 MEIN KYA BADLA (sirf app.js + version files) ===
app.js:
 * cashCandidates(): window 3 din se 7 din. partyId ki shart WAISE HI hai.
 * NAYA otherPartyCandidates(pos): doosri party ki cash purchase — sirf jab
   raqam BILKUL barabar ho, 3 din ke andar ho, aur sirf MALIK ko dikhti hai.
 * NAYA pickCandidates(pos) = same-party + (malik ho to) other-party.
 * autoLinkPurchases(): ab sirf KAMYABI par autoLinkTried mein id jati hai;
   nakami par notice() se wajah screen par dikhti hai aur console.warn hota
   hai. Auto-link ab bhi SIRF same-party + exact raqam par hota hai (mehfooz).
 * Party ka khata kholte hi bhi autoLinkPurchases() chalta hai (line ~145).
 * renderPurchases(): har un-linked POS bill par "Milao" button HAMESHA
   dikhta hai (count sifar ho to bhi). Banner ab pickCandidates par.
 * linkForm(): do hisson mein list — (a) isi party ki cash purchase,
   (b) "Doosri party ki barabar raqam" (party ka naam laal mein likha hua).
   (b) chunne par confirm() se dono naam dikha kar poocha jata hai.
   Candidate bilkul na ho to saaf paigham: "7 din ke andar is party ki koi
   khaali cash purchase nahi mili… (ya us par pehle se POS bill juda hai)".
 * error(): permission-denied ka paigham behtar — ab dono wajah likhta hai
   (record POS mein POSTED hai, ya naye firestore.rules publish nahi hue).
version.json / sw.js / index.html: 1.79.1 -> 1.79.2

  Agar "Ijazat nahi mili" phir bhi aaye to pehle Unpost karein.
- Party merge / naam se matching: NAHI ki (upar wajah dekhein).
- posBill field ka matlab wahi hai: cash-purchase entry par POS entry ki id.

=== AGLI DAFA DEKHNE WALI CHEEZEIN ===

=== v1.79.2 — DOOSRA HISSA: firestore.rules (usi chat mein baad mein) ===
WAJAH: malik ko "Ijazat nahi mili" is liye aa raha tha ke rules ki generic
owner-update line malik ka har update rok deti hai jab resource.data.posStatus
== 2 (POS mein POSTED). Malik pehle "Aaj ke 10 bill post" dabata hai, phir
Milao karta hai — Milao cash-purchase entry par posBill field likhta hai, aur
POSTED hone ki wajah se wo write reject ho jata tha.
BADLAV (tang aur mehfooz):
 * firestore.rules: nayi function linkOnlyEdit() — POSTED blueKhata doc par
   update sirf tab jayez jab badalne wale keys sirf
   ['posBill','posDiffOk','updatedAt','updatedBy','rev'] hon.
   Raqam / tareekh / party / kind / deleted POSTED ke baad ab bhi band hain.
 * app.js save(): isPosted(existing) wali rok ab isLinkOnlyChange(data,existing)
   ke sath — yani app bhi POSTED entry par sirf link jorne / alag karne deta hai.
   Nayi helper: isLinkOnlyChange() (autoLinkTried ke bilkul upar).
NOTE: rules mein aur kuch nahi chhua. Agar aage koi aur "Ijazat nahi mili"
aaye to pehle app ka naya paigham parhein (wajah likhta hai), andaze se rules
mat badlein.

=== TOAST KAHAN SE AATA HAI (2026-09-20, poora repo dekhne ke baad) ===
Malik ko "Ijazat nahi mili" Daily-sheet (Sale / Wasooli / Akhrajat) wali
screen par dikh raha tha, halanke us screen par kuch save nahi kiya tha.
Wajah: optimisticEdit() ka failure handler (app.js ~line 163) error() ko
ASYNC call karta hai. Yani Milao / link ka nakam write jis bhi screen par
banda us waqt khara ho, wahan toast dikha deta hai. Screen ka us error se
koi taalluq nahi hota — agli dafa isi dhoke mein na aayein.

=== v1.79.2 — TEESRA HISSA: full-mulazim ka link (malik ne kaha "problem mulazim ki entry par") ===
Bug: firestore.rules ki staffEditEntry() (blueStaff = Full App mulazim) ki
affectedKeys list mein 'posBill' aur 'posDiffOk' the hi nahi. purchaseStaff
wali line (~250) mein yeh v1.71 se mojood thay. Natija: FULL login wale
mulazim ke device par Milao / auto-milan hamesha permission-denied deta tha.
Fix: staffEditEntry() ki list mein 'posBill','posDiffOk' shamil kiye.
Baqi sab waise hi: aaj ki entry, dayOpen, rev+1, posStatus != 2.

=== v1.79.3 — CACHE WALI GHALTI (zaroori sabaq) ===
1.79.2 ke andar app.js DO dafa badla gaya, magar version number wahi raha.
sw.js ka CACHE naam ('sam-blue-khata-1.79.2') aur index.html ke ?v=1.79.2
bhi wahi rahe — is liye phone purana app.js hi cache se uthata raha aur malik
ko purana error message dikhta raha (isi se pata chala ke naya code nahi chala).
SABAQ: app.js / sale.js / pos-stock.js waghera mein zara bhi tabdeeli ho to
version.json + sw.js ka CACHE + index.html ke ?v= HAR DAFA barhana zaroori hai,
warna update phone tak pahunchta hi nahi.
1.79.3 = 1.79.2 ka poora kaam + naya tafseeli error message (failDetail).

=== v1.79.4 — error ki tafseel (tashkhees jari hai) ===
Malik ko 1.79.3 par paigham mila: "Ijazat nahi mili · record ka pata nahi
(background write)". Matlab nakami aise raste se aayi jo error() ko payload
deta hi nahi tha. 1.79.4 mein:
 * save() ka create-path (cloud.add) ab error(e,payload) bhejta hai.
 * sendPost (post / unpost / aaj ke bill) bhi context bhejta hai.
 * error() Firestore ka asal message bhi (80 harf) sath dikhata hai.
 * window 'unhandledrejection' par bhi permission-denied pakra jata hai.
AGLI AI: malik se poora toast text mangein — usi se pata chalega ke kaunsa
collection / field fail ho raha hai. Andaze se rules mat badlein.

=== v1.79.5 — ASAL MASLA MIL GAYA: "Din band" (dayLock) ===
Malik ne naye tafseeli error ka screenshot bheja:
 "Ijazat nahi mili · entry · collection · Rs 2 · 2026-09-20 · <party> · khula ·
  mulazim login · badle: · Missing or insufficient permissions"
Yani: POS / POSTED ka is se koi taalluq nahi tha. Mulazim (full scope) ki
AAJ ki normal entry Firestore par reject ho rahi thi.
WAJAH: rules ki har blueStaff line mein dayOpen() shart hai — din band ho to
mulazim ka koi write nahi. App ki taraf watchDayLock() SIRF EK DAFA chalta tha,
aur wo bhi cloud connect ke foran baad (login se pehle). Us waqt listen nakam
ho jaye (auth nahi hui / rules) to fail callback stopDayLock=null kar deta tha
aur dobara koshish kabhi nahi hoti thi. Natija: app samajhta raha ke din khula
hai (dayLock={}), UI ne entry karne di, aur Firestore ne mana kar diya —
wajah batae baghair.
FIX (app.js):
 * watchDayLock() ab onSession (login) par bhi chalta hai, aur nakami par
   8 second baad khud dobara koshish karta hai.
 * naya flag dayLockReady (aage kaam aayega).
 * failDetail(): agar din band hai aur full mulazim login hai to seedha likhta
   hai "AAJ KA DIN BAND HAI — malik Din wapas kholein dabaye".
FORI HAL MALIK KE LIYE: apne login se "🔓 Din wapas kholein" dabayein, phir
mulazim entry kar sakega.

=== v1.79.6 — LOGIN CHECK (tashkhees ka auzaar) ===
Mulazim ki entry par "Missing or insufficient permissions" jari raha,
halanke: din KHULA tha (malik ke button par "Din band karein" likha tha),
malik ne "Mulazim password" dobara Save bhi kiya, aur POSTED ka mamla bhi
nahi tha. Andaze khatam — is liye app mein hi jaanch daali gayi.
NAYA: Settings (⋮) mein "🔎 Login check" button (malik aur mulazim dono ko).
 * cloud.js: naya diagnose() — blueStaffSessions/{uid}, blueStaffKeys/{credentialId}
   aur blueAccess/dayLock parh kar wapas karta hai (har doc ka error alag).
 * app.js: runLoginCheck() sab kuch modal mein dikhata hai + natija likhta hai
   (key doc mila? active===true? scope=='full'? dayLock.closed aaj ka to nahi?).
RULES KA ASOOL (yaad rahe): blueStaff() = isStaff() && staffAccount().active == true
&& (scope na ho ya 'full'). staffAccount() DO get karta hai: session doc se
credentialId, phir blueStaffKeys/{credentialId}. Login ki apni sharayit narm hain
(active !== false chalta hai), is liye login ho jata hai magar write ruk sakta hai.
AGLI AI: pehle mulazim ke phone par Login check chalwayein aur uska screenshot
mangein. Us ke baghair rules ya app mat badlein.

=== v1.79.7 — mulazim ka Settings modal khamoshi se toot raha tha ===
1.79.6 mein "Login check" button to dikha, magar dabane par kuch nahi hota tha.
WAJAH: $('settings').onclick ke andar modal() ke foran baad MALIK-ONLY elements
istemal hote hain (restore, restoreSave, imports, syncSuppliers, staffPass,
changePass, trashBtn). Mulazim ke login par ye elements render hi nahi hote,
to pehla hi `restoreFile.onchange=` null par TypeError phenk deta tha aur us ke
baad ka saara code (button handlers samet) chalta hi nahi tha. Yeh purana bug
hai — sirf ab zahir hua kyunki naya button us ke baad laga tha.
FIX: (1) Login check ab document-level delegation se chalta hai (#loginCheck),
(2) upar wale saat malik-only handlers ko if($('id')) se guard kar diya.

=== v1.79.8 — Login check mein nakam payload ===
1.79.7 ka Login check chala. Natija: login record active:true + scope full,
session doc theek, din KHULA. Yani blueStaff() aur dayOpen() dono theek hain —
phir bhi create reject ho raha hai. Is liye ab aakhri nakam write ka POORA
payload (window.lastFail) Login check ke neeche JSON mein dikhaya jata hai
(pictures chhupa kar). Usi se pata chalega ke kaun sa field rule tor raha hai
(by / updatedBy / rev / type / kind / amount / date / koi extra field).

=== v1.79.9 — ★ ASAL JAR (root cause) MIL GAYI ★ ===
Login check ke "Aakhri nakam write" JSON se sab saaf ho gaya:
  auth ka asal uid : UkPw3L1KfBff56F5sQAQuDZMQo73   (diagnose se)
  entry par likha  : "by"/"updatedBy": "PtyC2G7Ol8X4bhmLMtQDV29TUF12"
Rules ki shart (blueKhata staff create): request.resource.data.by == request.auth.uid
Dono alag the -> permission-denied. POS / POSTED / dayLock / staff password —
in mein se koi wajah nahi thi (sab check ho chuke thay, sab theek nikle).
KYUN: app ka `session` object purane (khatam ho chuke) anonymous login ka uid
pakde hue tha. Anonymous user dobara ban gaya (naya uid) magar session update
nahi hua, aur save() payload mein session.user.uid hi likhta tha.
FIX:
 * cloud.js: naya currentUid() — auth.currentUser.uid seedha deta hai.
 * app.js save(): payload ka by/updatedBy ab LIVE uid se (cloud.currentUid()),
   aur agar session ka uid purana ho to session usi waqt naye uid par set ho
   jata hai (console.warn ke sath).
 * Login check ke natije mein uid ka farq bhi likha jata hai.
AGLI AI: "Missing or insufficient permissions" par sab se pehle uid ka milan
dekhein (Login check). Yeh ghalti dobara ho sakti hai agar kahin bhi
session.user.uid ko write payload mein seedha istemal kiya jaye — hamesha
cloud.currentUid() istemal karein.

=== v1.79.10 — abhi bhi ruka hua (jaanch jari) ===
1.79.9 (live uid) ke baad bhi mulazim ki entry par permission-denied.
Nakam payload: by = updatedBy = WANM5DyRbxUb8gloVve9gDV33gm2 (yani live uid
ab sahi likha ja raha hai). Matlab ab shak blueStaffSessions/{uid} par hai:
har staff login signInAnonymously se NAYA uid banata hai (auth-controller.js
line ~126) aur usi waqt createPasswordSession() session doc likhta hai. Agar
kisi wajah se wo doc us naye uid ke liye maujood na ho (ya kisi doosri tab ne
login badal diya ho), to rules ki staffAccount() = get(blueStaffSessions/{uid})
fail kar jati hai aur har write ruk jata hai — sab kuch warna theek dikhta hai.
NAYA is version mein:
 * Login check ke neeche khulasa: uid abhi vs payload ka by (mel ✅/❌) aur
   session doc mila ya nahi — scroll kiye baghair nazar aata hai.
 * checkSessionDoc(): login ke 2.5 second baad khud chalta hai; session doc ya
   active key na mile to foran saaf paigham deta hai.
NOTE: malik ke phone par 5 browser tabs khuli hui thin. Firebase auth ek hi
browser mein share hota hai — ek tab ka naya login doosri tab ka uid badal
deta hai. Malik ko keh diya gaya hai ke sirf EK tab rakhein.

=== 2026-09-20 sham — NATIJA: ghalti DEPLOYED rules mein hai, app mein nahi ===
Mulazim ke phone ka Login check (v1.79.10) — sab ✅:
  uid (auth)  = payload.by = payload.updatedBy   (mel khata hai)
  session doc = {credentialId, createdAt} mojood
  key doc     = {name:"Mulazim", scope:"full", active:true, loginEnabled:true}
  dayLock     = khula;  payload: type entry, kind collection, amount 200 (int),
  date aaj, rev 1, na 'deleted' na 'transferId'.

ki HAR shart is payload par poori hoti hai — phir bhi permission-denied.
Is ka ek hi matlab: Firebase console mein jo rules PUBLISHED hain wo repo wali
file jaise nahi (purana version / adhoora paste). Malik ko phone par rules

HAL: ek-button copy page banaya (rules-copy.html, claude.ai artifact) — malik

AGLI AI: agar phir bhi masla ho to malik se console ke Rules tab ki pehli

=== v1.79.11 — RULES BISECT PROBE (meri pichhli theory ghalat nikli) ===
Malik ne Firebase console ke Rules tab ke screenshot bheje: rules v1.79.2
PUBLISHED hain aur repo wali file se harf-ba-harf milte hain (project
note-traders-khata, Database default). Yani "rules publish nahi hue" ghalat tha.
Ab soorat: deployed rules = repo rules; payload har shart poori karta hai;
login record theek; din khula — phir bhi mulazim ki create permission-denied.
cloud.add() sada setDoc hai (koi batch / cashRevision sath nahi jata).
Is liye sharton ko alag-alag azmane ka probe banaya (cloud.js probe()):
 1. getDocFromServer(blueAccess/cashRevision)  -> sirf blueStaff()
 2. getDocFromServer(blueAccess/dayLock)
 3. create expenseAccount "ZZ TEST" -> blueStaff+dayOpen+by/updatedBy/rev (tareekh ke baghair)
 4. create entry kind expense amount 1, date = aaj (PKT) -> + isToday + amount/kind
 + server ka waqt (version.json ke Date header se) aur phone ka waqt.
Login check -> "🧪 Rules test chalayein". Natije se pata chalega kaunsi shart
tooti. Test "ZZ TEST" naam ke 1-2 record bana sakta hai — malik delete kar de.

=== v1.79.12 — ★ GHALIB WAJAH: Firestore ki 1000-expression hadd ★ ===
Sab kuch check ho chuka tha aur theek tha: deployed rules = repo rules (console
ke screenshot), login record active+full, uid = by = updatedBy, din khula,
payload har shart poori karta hai, cloud.add sada setDoc. Phir bhi mulazim ki
create par permission-denied; malik ki entry theek.
FARQ: malik ki request generic block ki pehli allow par pass ho jati hai (sasti).
Mulazim ki request blueKhata ki SAATON allow-create lines chalwati hai (transfer,
purchase x2, closing, custody, aam entry) aur har ek blueStaff()/purchaseStaff()
bulati hai. Purana blueStaff() staffAccount() ko 3 dafa chalata tha (har dafa 2
get + lambe path). Firestore: "max 1,000 expressions evaluated per request" —
hadd paar ho to sirf permission-denied milta hai, wajah nahi. Lines har version
ke sath barhti gayin (v1.61 transfer, cashCustody, v1.78 posStatus…), is liye
"update ke baad" shuru hua.
FIX (firestore.rules, matlab wahi):
 * acctIs(a, scope) — staffAccount() ab fi helper EK dafa evaluate hota hai.
 * create lines par sasti shart pehle: 'transferId' in data / purchase==true /
   type=='party' / 'closing' / 'cashCustody'; aam entry wali line mein type aur
   !transferId pehle, phir blueStaff().
 * update lines (closing / cashCustody / party) par type ki shart pehle.
NOTE: yeh natija mantiqi tor par nikala gaya hai (emulator yahan nahi chal
sakta). Agar publish ke baad bhi masla rahe to Login check -> "Rules test
chalayein" ka screenshot mangein — wo bataega kaunsi shart tooti.
AGLI AI: rules mein nayi allow line daalte waqt SASTI shart pehle rakhein aur
blueStaff()/purchaseStaff() ko kam se kam bulayein.

=== v1.79.12 KA NATIJA + v1.79.13 ===
v1.79.12 ke halke rules PUBLISH hote hi mulazim ki entry save hone lagi —
yani 1000-expression hadd wali tashkhees DURUST thi. (Malik: "ho gayi hai".)
v1.79.13: malik ne kaha "same day mein mulazim edit kar sakta hai, yahan nahi
ho raha". Wajah app ke andar tazaad:
 * canEditEntry(e) (button dikhata hai) aur rules ka staffEditEntry() dono
   full mulazim ko AAJ ki entry edit karne dete hain,
 * magar save() ke non-owner guard mein sirf purchase / cashCustody / closing /
   soft-delete ki istisna thi — aam entry ka edit chhoot gaya tha, is liye
   "Sirf malik edit kar sakta hai" aata tha.
FIX: save() mein nayi istisna — existing.type==='entry' && canEditEntry(existing)
&& existing.date===today() && data.date===today(). Rules nahi chhue.
AGAR edit par "Ijazat nahi mili" aaye: rules ki update lines 251 / 311 / 334
(blueKhata) par bhi sasti shart pehle lagani hogi (type=='entry', purchase,
deleted) — wohi 1000-expression wala masla update par bhi ho sakta hai.

=== v1.80.0 — AI ke BAGHAIR teen behtariyan (malik ke OK ke baad) ===
Pas-e-manzar: malik ne poocha app mein AI se kya behtar ho sakta hai. 9 tajaweez
di gayin; malik ne no.4 (WhatsApp wasooli paigham) aur no.8 (rozana khulasa)
MANA kar diye. Tay shuda tarteeb:
  v1.80  = no.6 + 7 + 9 (yeh version, AI nahi)
  v1.81  = no.3 sham ka milan: kaapi ki "Sale + Roznamcha pictures" ko AI (Google
           Gemini) se parhwa kar Daily Sale se milana. Malik ke faisle: key
           Firestore ke doc mein (malik + Full App mulazim parh saken), dono chala
           saken, natija din ke sath save ho, kuch khud na badle. Rules ki 2 nayi
           lines + Publish chahiye. Malik ko Gemini key banani hai.
  phir   = no.1 purchase bill ki photo se entry, no.2 bol kar entry, no.5 khate se sawal.
IS VERSION MEIN (sirf app.js + style.css; rules NAHI chhue):
 (6) oddAmountMessage(data,existing) — save() ke andar, duplicate check ke baad.
     Usi party (ya kharche ke account) + usi kind ki pichhli 20 entries; kam az
     kam 4 hon. median se 8x zyada aur range se bahar (ya 8x kam) ho to confirm().
     Raqam na badli ho (link / POS / soft-delete) to check nahi chalta.
 (7) similarParties(name,phone,skipId) + similarHTML() — accountForm ke Save par.
     nameKey() = norm + harf/hindse ke siwa sab hata do. score 4 naam wohi,
     3 mobile (aakhri 10 hindse) wohi, 2 ek naam doosre se shuru, 1 edit-distance<=2.
     Pehli dafa Save par dabba dikhta hai aur button "Phir bhi naya banao" ban
     jata hai; doosri dafa Save ho jata hai. MERGE KABHI NAHI (malik ka hukum:
     Saim purchaser / SAIM purchaser / Saim default… sab ALAG suppliers hain).
 (9) paintDueNudge() — renderSurface() ke shuru mein. #dueNudge section
     #dailyBackupReminder se pehle JS se banta hai; all('reminder') se aaj due /
     late ginta hai; sirf view==='khata' && !selected par; malik + full mulazim.
Helpers ka unit test node se chalaya gaya (28,000 vs 2-3.5 hazaar -> tanbeeh;
"saim purchaser" -> dono Saim khate; "Tarik bhawal" -> Tariq bhawal).

=== v1.81.0 — SHAM KA MILAN (AI: Google Gemini) — malik ke OK ke baad ===
Maqsad: malik sham ko haath ki kaapi (roznamcha) ko app ki Daily Sale se milata
hai (pehle ChatGPT mein tasveerein daal kar karta tha). Ab app ke andar.
FAISLE (malik ke): key Firestore doc mein; malik + Full App mulazim dono chala
saken; natija save ho; kuch khud na badle. WhatsApp paigham aur rozana khulasa
MANA hain.
NAYI FILE ai-tally.js (pure, node se test shuda):
 * shrinkForAI(file,1800px,q .85) — AI ke liye BARI tasveer (save nahi hoti;
   dayPhoto wali 900px tasveerein hindson ke liye dhundli hain).
 * pickModel(key) — GET /v1beta/models se behtareen "gemini-X-flash" chunta hai
   (Google naam badalta rehta hai; model ka naam hard-code NA karein).
 * readPages({key,model,images}) — POST /v1beta/models/{model}:generateContent,
   header x-goog-api-key, parts: inline_data + TALLY_PROMPT,
   generationConfig {temperature:0,responseMimeType:'application/json'}.
 * parseItems(text) -> [{amount(rupay,int),text,struck,unsure}]
 * matchTally(book,rows,totals): 1) bilkul barabar 2) kaapi mein likha total
   (Sale/Wasooli/Akhrajat/change/cash) 3) milti-julti = ek hindsa badla / ek
   hindsa zyada-kam (x10) / do hindse aage-peeche. Milan RAQAM par hota hai,

=== v1.99.0 — AI KO "SIKHANA": BILL KA NAAM -> SYSTEM KA ITEM (alias se; rules wahi v1.94) ===
MALIK KI FARMAISH: "kya hum AI ko train kar sakte hain — bill ke naam aur PC ke naam yaad kar le, aur milan wali

Naya collection NAHI, nayi rules NAHI. Faida dogana: smartSearch har jagah (Sale/Stock/Purchase) usi naam se
item dhoondh leta hai.

app.js:
- import mein aliasOf shamil (smart-search.js).
- learnAlias(itemId,name): naam saaf (comma/newline hata kar, 60 harf tak) -> aliasOf(itemId) ki CSV mein jorta hai
  (pehle se ho to 'pehle se'), CSV 120 harf se barhe to sab se purana naam nikal deta hai (Stock ke input ka
  maxlength 120 hai), phir cloud.setAlias(itemId, csv). Return: 'ok' | 'pehle se' | paigham.
  permission-denied -> "naam sirf malik yaad karwa sakta hai (rules)" — yani MULAZIM ke liye rules badalni parengi
  (aliases collection par staff write). Malik ke liye abhi bhi kaam karta hai.
- photoCompareHTML (milan): har jori par, agar POS line ka itemId ho, "✓ Yeh naam yaad kar lo" button
  (data-tally-learn="<itemId>|<AI ka naam>"); alag click handler (learnAlias ke natije ke mutabiq button ki jagah
  chhoti line likh deta hai).
- ppSetup ko naya hook: learn: learnAlias.

purchase.js (AI wale bill ki screen):
- aiRows[] = {ai (AI ki line), key (cart line ka k), itemId, learn}. aiItems() sirf data banata hai; dikhana
  aiRender() karta hai (dialog dobara banata hai, is liye har tabdeeli par nayi list).
- Har mili hui line: "bill ka naam → system ka naam" + [✓ Yaad kar lo] (aiLearn -> learnOf) + [✏️ Badlein].
  Na mili line: [✏️ Item chunein].
- aiPickForm(i): search + list (smartSearch), "‹ Wapas list par" (data-pp-aiback). aiPicked(i,id): purani cart line
  hata kar naya item daalta hai, AI ki ginti/rate aiApply() se lagata hai, aur PHIR KHUD aiLearn() chala deta hai
  (yani item chunna = naam sikhana).
- aiApply(ln,l): wahi usool — line par ctn ho aur item ka pack ho to rate = FI CTN (costP = rate/pack), warna fi pcs.
- Naye data-* : pp-learn, pp-pick, pp-picked ("i|itemId"), pp-aiback — click handler mein pp-photo/pp-ai ke foran baad.

=== v2.4.0 — JAANCH MODE + LINE KA DIMAGH (rules wahi v2.2 — kuch publish nahi) ===
MALIK KI BAAT (Affan + Bhaiya ke bills, badam/dania screenshot, Godam 1 aur 3 ki stock PDF ke baad): "AI bill samajh nahi
pata — pcs/carton; badam ka carton nahi aata; ek item ke rates lagte hi agla khud aaye, upar neeche na jana pare."
MALIK KE FAISLE: hari lines EK TAP par pakki, baqi ek ek dikhao. Khareed rate = bill ka Rate (Amt ÷ ginti nahi).
Bonus nahi aata. PC ka hissa (pack-lagao.js — POS mein Items.PackQty bharna) BAAD mein, jab malik PC ki files bheje:
purchase-post.js, rate-lagao.js, sync-stock.js, start-all .bat (node_modules / firebase-key.json NAHI).
PC ka purana khata-sync.zip (22 Sep) is chat mein tha: POS ka carton size = dbo.Items.PackQty (+ PackQtyName);
rate-lagao.js wala job pattern (claim -> transaction -> SystemNotes -> done/failed) naye script ka namuna hai.

LINE KA DIMAGH (purchase.js, "v2.4.0: LINE KA DIMAGH + JAANCH MODE" block)
- jCandidates(ln, ai): pcs {ginti = pieces, rate fi piece} · ctn {POS pack > 1: ginti = carton, rate ÷ pack} ·
  both {ctn aur pcs dono bhare: "5+3"} · size {bill ka سائز (ya yaad kiya memOf(id).s) > 1 aur POS pack se alag:
  pieces = ginti × size, khareed = rate ÷ size}.
- jJudge(r, fmt): har candidate ka score = |ln(khareed / pichhla khareed)| + naqsha ki tarjeeh (-0.3) (+ r.force -100).
  Pichhla khareed = baseOf (itemRates memory ya POS prate). Kuch bhi maloom na ho -> purana andaza.
  RANG: ±10% = 🟢 g · ±50% = 🟡 y ("rate X% badla") · us se door = 🔴 r ("N guna — unit / ginti check").
  Pichhla khareed na ho = 🟡. PEHRE: hisaab (ginti × rate vs line total, 20% gunjaish tax/discount ke liye) -> 🔴;
  stock (khareed > max(stock×10, pack×40 ya 400)) -> 🔴 "Stock ka pehra: X se Y". Item naam ke andaze se (byAi false)
  mila ho to hara nahi, zyada se zyada 🟡.
- jAutoFmt(): hari lines ke modes se naqsha KHUD (pcs / ctn / both — size aur both 'ctn' gine jate hain) aur save.
  Patti sirf tab jab pakra na ja sake. Patti par TAP = IS bill par r.force (sab lines), agle bills par tarjeeh.

JAANCH UI
- aiRender() = overview: ginti (🟢/🟡/🔴), bill total vs lines + farq, naqsha line (khud pakra / badlein), "✓ N hari
  pakki karein" (jAccept: done + naam yaad), "Jaanch shuru (N) ›", aur har line ka jz-row (tap = card).
- jCard(i): bill ki tasveer (chhoti, ⤢), "Bill par" (naam, ginti, سائز, ریٹ, کل), "App ne lagaya" (item, unit, khareed,
  pichhla + farq), wajahen, unit chips (data-pp-jmode = r.force), khane: CTN/PCS/khareed/W/R (seedha cart line mein,
  khareed badle to pichhla nafa se W/R), [✓ Theek — agla ›] [Item badlein] [✕ Chhor do] [‹ List].
  jNext(): pehle ghair-hari, phir baqi; khatam = jSummary().
- jSummary(): bill ka farq (aur andaza: tax / T.O. / mazdoori), mehnge / saste items (rupay fi unit), do dafa item
  "Mila do" (jMerge: ginti jama, khareed wazni ausat), chhori lines "↺ wapas" (jUnskip), laal baqi ki yaad-dahani,
  "💾 POS mein bhejo" (seedha save()).
- Bhejne ke baad aiRows = null (purani jaanch band — pehle bheje hue bill ki jaanch dobara khul sakti thi).
- Enter / Done: .sale-cart aur .jc-card mein agla khana; line badle to scrollIntoView; card ke aakhri khane par ✓.

SIKHNA (save par)
- learnFromBill(): naqsha (modes se), r (q ke mutabiq), n, b (bills), g (hari ka ausat %), ex = 12 misalein
  "bill ka naam => humara item | tareeqa x size". app.js billNote() yeh misalein aur 'both' ki hidayat AI ko deta hai.
- itemRates memory ab s (carton size) bhi rakhti hai (sizeMemFromBill). cloud.js setItemRates s, setBillFormat
  g/b/ex rakhta hai (rules mein map ki values par shart nahi — rules badalne ki zarurat nahi).
- packGaps() / packsView(): "📦 Carton size khali (N)" — jin items ka bill par سائز mila magar POS mein pack khali
  ya alag. Abhi sirf dikhata hai; pack-lagao.js ke baad yahin se bharna.

AI (ai-tally.js): naya 'size' khana (سائز / Size / Packing / Wt) + hidayat: "من ریٹ" (fi 40 kg) ko rate mat samjho —
rate wohi jo ginti se zarb ho kar line ka total bane. parseBill size rakhta hai.

TEST (node + jsdom, ASLI BILLS KE NUMBER): runj.mjs 23 — Bhaiya: badam 5 کارٹن × سائز 23 = 115 @ 2,859.65 🟢, dania
5 CTN @ 575 🟢, ajwain 10 @ 325 🟢, khali bori 🔴, naqsha khud "Dono", hari pakki, khali bori chhori, POS ko 3 lines,

  hai (natural size se height nikal kar background-position), .jc-mark peela nishan line par. y na ho to jY() padosi

- sizeMemFromBill: pehle r.cs, phir r.j.size — sirf un items ka jin ka POS pack khali (itemRates.s).

Rules NAHI badlin (v2.2 hi). Files: purchase.js, ai-tally.js, cloud.js, style.css + version bump.
1) jFix(r, ln): hisaab (ginti × rate = kul, 2%) na mile to:

   ya bill par سائز 1 (aur bill rate pichhle khareed se ±50%). Band = sirf PCS, ek ginti, bill ka سائز carton nahi.
   Bill ka سائز carton size SIRF jab app ne 'size' tareeqa chuna ho (khajoor 1000 gram carton nahi banta).
   itemRates s=1 = band (cloud.js ab s>=1 rakhta hai; packGaps s>1 hi dikhata hai).

   na mile -> peela note. (Bhaiya: khajoor 15500÷40=387.5 ✓, sufi 20400÷40×20=10200 ✓)

   "✓ Hisaab bill se mila" note jab line ki raqam bill se 0.5% ke andar.

==================== v2.18.0 (2026-09-26) — TRANSFER NOTE KI SMART SEARCH ====================
Rules NAHI badlin (2.10). Files: smart-search.js, pos-stock.js, style.css + version files.

=== MASLA (malik ne screenshot bheja) ===
Transfer note (godam se godam) ki search mein "Mayo" likhne par "mop 500" bhi aa raha tha,
aur 0 stock wale item (Youngs mayo 200ml — Godam 1 mein 0) upar list mein jagah gher rahe the.
MALIK KA FAISLA: jis godam SE maal nikal raha hai, wahan 0 stock wale item BILKUL CHUPA DO.

=== smart-search.js ===
* scoreItem() ab { sc, fz } deta hai. fz = true jab match SIRF ed1 (1 harf ki ghalti) se bana ho.
  partyScore() ko .sc lagana para — agli AI dhyan rakhe, ye function ab object deta hai.
* smartSearch(items, q, limit, opts) — naya chautha parameter (purani sab callein waise hi chalti hain):
    opts.keep(r)  -> false to item scan hi nahi hota
    opts.bonus(r) -> extra score (transfer mein "aksar bheje jane wale" ko halka upar lane ke liye)
* NAYA USOOL: pakke match >= 3 hon to andaza (fz) wale match BILKUL nahi lautte.
  Isi se "mayo" par "mop" khatam. Pakka match na ho to fuzzy pehle jaisa chalta hai (mayonaise -> Mayonise).
  YE USOOL SARI APP PAR HAI (sale + purchase + transfer) — jaan bujh kar, kyunki faalto result har jagah masla the.
* hlName(text, q) — match hue harf <mark> mein, andar se khud escape karta hai (esc() dobara na lagayein).

=== pos-stock.js (sirf openTransfer wala hissa) ===
* trHas(r) = trStockOf(trFrom, r.id) > 0.0005. Search ka natija do hisson mein: ok / zero.
  Default sirf ok dikhte hain; zero ki ginti neeche patti par "phir bhi dikhayein" (trZeroShow = true).
  trZeroShow har naye harf par aur openTransfer() par false ho jata hai.
* NOTE: 0-stock chhupana SIRF transfer ki search mein hai. Stock screen / sale / purchase ka
  purana usool (stock ka tarteeb par asar nahi) waise hi hai — usay mat badlein.
* Barcode: /^[0-9]{8,}$/ aur code ya bc[] se BILKUL barabar mile -> trPick(item, true):
  item lag gaya, search khana khali, cursor us line ke Ctn (ya Pcs) khane par, value select.
  8 se kam hindse = normal search (chhote codes jaise 165465 galti se auto na lag jayein).
* trFocus = {id, qty} module var. openTransfer() ke aakhir mein: qty true ho to us line ka khana,
  warna wapas search input — isi liye ek ke baad ek item likhna tez ho gaya.
* Recent: localStorage 'sam-tr-recent-v1' (aakhri 15 id). trRecNote() search-pick, click aur
  camera scan teenon par. Khali khane par upar 6 dikhte hain (sirf jin ka us godam mein stock hai).
  trRecRank() sirf (15-i)/6 ka halka bonus deta hai — match ki jagah nahi leta.
* Rows ki HTML: trRowHTML() -> .sh-row > .sh-name (hlName) + .sh-chips (godam / stock / pack / code).
  Stock chip: laal 0, peela (carton wale par 1 carton se kam), warna hara.
* Keyboard: ⬆⬇ trMark() se .on class, Enter = .on wala (ya pehla), Esc = band. Search 70ms debounce.

=== style.css (file ke aakhir mein) ===
.scan-hits.sh-hits ... .sh-row / .sh-name mark / .sh-chip (godam, green, amber, red, pack, code) / .sh-more.
Class SIRF transfer ke hits par lagti hai (hits.classList.add('sh-hits')), is liye sale/purchase ki
purani dropdown bilkul nahi badli.

=== JAANCH ===
Node par smartSearch ka alag test: "Mayo" -> 3 mayo items (mop gaya), "mayonaise" -> Mayonise,
"1kg mayo" -> Mayo national 1kg, "13213" -> 500gm, 0-stock chhupne par hidden=1, barcode 8964000061107 -> Youngs.

==================== App v2.96.0 (2026-10-07) — 🚚 GAARI v4 (farzi diesel, tanki khata, qist) + ⚡ cash/transfer Save foran ====================
User ki baat: diesel tanki full km ke hisaab se; har phere ka diesel FARZI (Falcon km ÷ 5.5 × rate) tanki khata mein jama, phere ke total mein
asal diesel plus/minus NAHI; dobara full par asal nafe se cash kat kar diesel; tanki khata mein farzi vs asal farq; farq MARK = asal average
app mein lag jaye. Qist 140,000 + fixed salary nafe se nikal kar tay ho NAFA ya GHATA. Mobil 5000 km. Tanki 76 L. Parchi par litre + rate.
* gaari.js:
  - pc(): avg = p.dAvg || avgFor(phere ka waqt), rate = p.dRate || rateFor(phere ka waqt); shouldL/shouldRs = farzi; nafa = kiraya − comm − kh − farzi
    (asal dL/dRs sirf dikhane ko). driverDe = kiraya − kh − comm; lena = driverDe − pehle le chuke (col); settled = 0.
  - rateFor(key): us waqt se pehle ki aakhri parchi ka rate, ya cfg.dieselRate agar cfg.rateAt us ke baad aur phere se pehle. curRate = rateFor(null).
  - avgFor(key): cfg.avgHist [{at,avg}] (pehli entry {at:0, purani avg}); "farq mark" / setting badalne par sirf BAAD ke pheron par nayi average —
    is liye mark karne se purana cycle ka farq zero nahi hota. Phera band par dRate/dAvg jamte (kholne par 0 = live).
  - fills(): phere ki diesel lines (key = pheraKey|zNNN, phere ke baad) + kind diesel docs (key = date|at). tankCycles(): full se full cycle —
    pheras key ke beech, beech ki "thora" fills jor; closed: actL = tops + end.l, farq = actRs − farziRs, avg = km / actL. Pehli full se pehle ke
    phere "before" (farzi nafe mein, milan mein nahi). tankNow: used = farziL − topL, left = cap − used, pct; needRs = used × curRate.
  - monthCalc: safi = kiraya − comm − kh − farzi; fixed (cfg.fixed [{n,a}]), tankFarq (is mahine khatam hone wale cycles), qist (cfg.qist);
    beforeQist = safi − fixed − tankFarq; qistPaid/qistBaqi; nafa = beforeQist − qist (day filter par fixed/qist nahi). driverBaqi − settled.comm.
  - UI: hero NAFA/GHATA + qist progress + flow chips; ⛽ tanki card (dial SVG + %, L, bharwana hai, odometer) -> Tanki khata modal
    (dashboard: dial + tanki SVG + meter digits + stats; "⛽ Tanki full karwa li" / "＋ Thora diesel"; cycles: farzi vs asal, farq, average,
    "✓ Farq mark karein"; diesel history). openFill: parchi photo AI {litre,rate,amount}, date+waqt (at), meter; owner par cfg.dieselRate/rateAt.
    🛢 chip + openOil (kind kharch oil:true, ginti phere km se, 90% peela, 100% laal). odoNow: aakhri reading/fill odo/mobil odo + baad ke km (≈).
    Phera page: farzi diesel box (rate na ho to ⚠️), "Rastay mein dalwaya" lines tanki khata mein, nichod BACHAT, 💰 Driver se lena
    (safi nafa + farzi), owner "✅ Driver se le liya" (sab bilty col + settled{at,amount,comm}), ↩ wapas. Setting: dieselRate, tankL 76, qist,
    oilKm 5000, fixed kharche rows. PDF naye hisaab se. Rules badalne ki zaroorat NAHI (diesel/kharch kinds, _config owner).
* app.js ⚡ (pending wala kaam poora):
  - save(): cashCustody ab await nahi (optimisticEdit peeche) — cloud.edit cash par getDocsFromServer(cashQuery) SAARI cash docs parhta tha
    (har din barhta) + transaction = 6s+. Server jaanch wahi; mana ho to wapas + error(). Doosri cash entry jaldi par saaf paigham.
  - commitAccountTransfer(request, {wait}): EDIT / DELETE ab peeche (run() background, "bg" return, notice; ghalti par error()). POS voucher
    wala raasta {wait:true} (list foran taza chahiye).
* Test: happy-dom naqli data — 200 km/5.5=36.4 L x280 = 10,182 farzi, asal 38 L 10,830, farq 648, avg 5.26, mark ke baad sirf naya phera 5.26;
  qist/fixed hero; settle; setting save; parchi AI fill + rate. (app.dom.test.mjs is mahol mein purane version par bhi nahi chalti — happy-dom farq.)
